Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the machine_learning structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 969. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 120 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 969 + O(1/n^120). Summing over all modes and applying the machine_learning convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
